- **scienceworld-conditional-placer**: Places an object into one of several designated containers based on a measured condition, such as a temperature threshold. - **scienceworld-container-inspector**: Inspects the contents of a container or device using the 'look at' action. - **scienceworld-container-transfer**: Moves a substance or object from one container to another (e.g., moving lead to a metal pot). - **scienceworld-liquid-filler**: This skill fills a container with liquid from a source by first placing the container at the source, then activating the source (e.g., turning on a sink), and finally deactivating it. - **scienceworld-liquid-pourer**: Transfers the contents of a source liquid container into a target container for mixing or preparation. - **scienceworld-liquid-source-finder**: This skill searches rooms for a liquid source (e.g., sink, toilet, jug) by sequentially surveying different locations. - **scienceworld-living-entity-identifier**: Analyzes room observations to identify potential living things (e.g., eggs, plants, animals) among listed objects. - **scienceworld-material-classifier**: This skill makes a determination about a material's property (e.g., conductivity) based on environmental cues or domain knowledge when direct testing fails. - **scienceworld-mixture-creator**: This skill chemically mixes the contents of a container using the 'mix' action. - **scienceworld-object-focuser**: This skill selects and focuses on a specific object to signal task intent or prepare it for manipulation. - **scienceworld-object-locator**: Searches for a specific target object across multiple rooms by systematically teleporting to likely locations and examining each room. - **scienceworld-object-placer**: Moves a specified object from the environment or inventory into a target container based on a classification decision. - **scientific-agent-skills-shap**: Explain and audit machine-learning predictions with SHAP.
